<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\AuditLog;

class AuditLogController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        // CCTV LOCK: Only System Owners OR Super Admins with explicit 'view_audit_logs' permission
        if (!auth()->user()->hasRole('System Owner') && !auth()->user()->hasPermissionTo('view_audit_logs')) {
            abort(403, 'ACCESS DENIED: You do not have security clearance to view the Global Audit Trail.');
        }

        // Fetch logs with relationships, newest first, 20 per page
        $logs = AuditLog::with(['admin', 'user'])->latest()->paginate(20);
        return view('admin.audit-logs.index', compact('logs'));
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Investment;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;

class InvestmentController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display a listing of the resource.
     */
    public function index()
    {
        $investments = Investment::with(['user', 'package'])->latest()->paginate(10);
        return view('admin.investments.index', compact('investments'));
    }

    /**
     * Approve the specified investment.
     */
    public function approve(Investment $investment)
    {
        if (!auth()->user()->hasAnyRole(['System Owner', 'Super Admin', 'super_admin']) && !auth()->user()->hasDirectPermission('manage_investments')) {
            abort(403, 'Unauthorized. You do not have the required permissions to manage investments.');
        }

        if ($investment->status !== 'pending') {
            return back()->with('error', 'Investment is already processed.');
        }

        $investment->status = 'active';
        
        // If start_date column exists, set it to now
        if (Schema::hasColumn('investments', 'start_date')) {
            $investment->start_date = now();
        }

        $investment->save();

        // Notify Investor (Try/Catch to ensure process continues even if email fails)
        try {
            \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Mail::to($investment->user->email)->send(new \App\Mail\InvestmentApproved($investment));
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log::error('Failed to send investment approval email: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }

        // Calculate Amount Dynamically
        $amount = $investment->package->price * $investment->units;

        // Non-Repudiation Logging (Guaranteed Execution)
        \App\Models\AuditLog::create([
            'user_id' => $investment->user_id,
            'admin_id' => auth()->id(),
            'action' => 'Investment Approved',
            'details' => "Approved investment of GH₵ " . number_format($amount, 2) . " for " . $investment->user->email,
        ]);

        return back()->with('success', 'Investment approved successfully.');
    }

    public function reject(Investment $investment)
    {
        if (!auth()->user()->hasAnyRole(['System Owner', 'Super Admin', 'super_admin']) && !auth()->user()->hasDirectPermission('manage_investments')) {
            abort(403, 'Unauthorized. You do not have the required permissions to manage investments.');
        }

        if ($investment->status !== 'pending') {
            return back()->with('error', 'Investment is already processed.');
        }

        $investment->status = 'rejected';
        $investment->save();

        // Calculate Amount Dynamically
        $amount = $investment->package->price * $investment->units;

        // Non-Repudiation Logging (Guaranteed Execution)
        \App\Models\AuditLog::create([
            'user_id' => $investment->user_id,
            'admin_id' => auth()->id(),
            'action' => 'Investment Rejected',
            'details' => "Rejected investment of GH₵ " . number_format($amount, 2) . ".",
        ]);

        return back()->with('success', 'Investment rejected successfully.');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Package;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

class PackageController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display a listing of the resource.
     */
    public function index()
    {
        // FORCE PAGINATION: Ensure we return a LengthAwarePaginator, not a Collection
        $packages = Package::latest()->paginate(10);
        return view('admin.packages.index', compact('packages'));
    }

    /**
     * Show the form for creating a new resource.
     */
    public function create()
    {
        if (!auth()->user()->hasAnyRole(['System Owner', 'Super Admin', 'super_admin']) && !auth()->user()->hasDirectPermission('manage_packages')) {
            abort(403, 'Unauthorized. You do not have the required permissions to manage packages.');
        }

        return view('admin.packages.create');
    }

    /**
     * Store a newly created resource in storage.
     */
    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        if (!auth()->user()->hasAnyRole(['System Owner', 'Super Admin', 'super_admin']) && !auth()->user()->hasDirectPermission('manage_packages')) {
            abort(403, 'Unauthorized. You do not have the required permissions to manage packages.');
        }

        $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'price' => 'nullable|numeric|min:0',
            'roi_percentage' => 'nullable|numeric|min:0',
            'duration_months' => 'nullable|integer|min:1',
            'status' => 'nullable|in:draft,open,closed,sold_out',
            'image' => 'nullable', 
            'image_url' => 'nullable|string',
            'description' => 'nullable|string',
        ]);

        $slug = Str::slug($request->name);
        $count = Package::where('slug', 'LIKE', "{$slug}%")->count();
        if ($count > 0) {
            $slug .= '-' . ($count + 1);
        }

        $data = $request->except(['image', 'image_url']);
        $data['slug'] = $slug;

        // 1. Handle File Upload (Native SDK)
        if ($request->hasFile('image') && $request->file('image')->isValid()) {
            // Fetch URL safely bypassing Laravel's config cache entirely
            $cloudUrl = $_SERVER['CLOUDINARY_URL'] ?? $_ENV['CLOUDINARY_URL'] ?? env('CLOUDINARY_URL');

            if (empty($cloudUrl) || str_contains($cloudUrl, '<your_api_key>')) {
                return back()->withErrors(['image' => 'Cloudinary is not configured. Please set a valid CLOUDINARY_URL in your .env file.']);
            }

            // Initialize Native PHP SDK directly
            $cloudinary = new \Cloudinary\Cloudinary($cloudUrl);

            // Execute upload
            $response = $cloudinary->uploadApi()->upload($request->file('image')->getRealPath(), [
                'folder' => 'castle_royal_packages'
            ]);

            $data['image'] = $response['secure_url'];
        } 
        // 2. Handle URL fallback
        elseif ($request->filled('image_url')) {
            $data['image'] = $request->image_url;
        }

        Package::create($data);
        
        // Non-Repudiation Logging (Package Creation)
        \App\Models\AuditLog::create([
            'user_id' => null, // System Action
            'admin_id' => auth()->id(),
            'action' => 'Package Created',
            'details' => "Created investment package '{$request->name}' with price GH₵ " . number_format($request->price ?? 0, 2),
        ]);

        return redirect()->route('admin.packages.index')->with('status', 'Package created successfully!');
    }

    /**
     * Show the form for editing the specified resource.
     */
    public function edit(Package $package)
    {
        if (!auth()->user()->hasAnyRole(['System Owner', 'Super Admin', 'super_admin']) && !auth()->user()->hasDirectPermission('manage_packages')) {
            abort(403, 'Unauthorized. You do not have the required permissions to manage packages.');
        }

        return view('admin.packages.edit', compact('package'));
    }

    /**
     * Update the specified resource in storage.
     */
    public function update(Request $request, Package $package)
    {
        if (!auth()->user()->hasAnyRole(['System Owner', 'Super Admin', 'super_admin']) && !auth()->user()->hasDirectPermission('manage_packages')) {
            abort(403, 'Unauthorized. You do not have the required permissions to manage packages.');
        }

        $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'price' => 'nullable|numeric|min:0',
            'roi_percentage' => 'nullable|numeric|min:0',
            'duration_months' => 'nullable|integer|min:1',
            'status' => 'nullable|in:draft,open,closed,sold_out',
            'image' => 'nullable',
            'image_url' => 'nullable|string',
            'description' => 'nullable|string',
        ]);

        if ($request->name !== $package->name) {
             $slug = Str::slug($request->name);
             $count = Package::where('slug', 'LIKE', "{$slug}%")->where('id', '!=', $package->id)->count();
             if ($count > 0) {
                 $slug .= '-' . ($count + 1);
             }
             $package->slug = $slug;
        }

        $package->fill($request->except(['image', 'image_url']));

        // 1. Handle File Upload (Native SDK)
        if ($request->hasFile('image') && $request->file('image')->isValid()) {
            // Fetch URL safely bypassing Laravel's config cache entirely
            $cloudUrl = $_SERVER['CLOUDINARY_URL'] ?? $_ENV['CLOUDINARY_URL'] ?? env('CLOUDINARY_URL');

            if (empty($cloudUrl) || str_contains($cloudUrl, '<your_api_key>')) {
                return back()->withErrors(['image' => 'Cloudinary is not configured. Please set a valid CLOUDINARY_URL in your .env file.']);
            }

            // Initialize Native PHP SDK directly
            $cloudinary = new \Cloudinary\Cloudinary($cloudUrl);

            // Execute upload
            $response = $cloudinary->uploadApi()->upload($request->file('image')->getRealPath(), [
                'folder' => 'castle_royal_packages'
            ]);

            $package->image = $response['secure_url'];
        } 
        // 2. Handle URL fallback
        elseif ($request->filled('image_url')) {
            $package->image = $request->image_url;
        }

        $package->save();

        // Non-Repudiation Logging (Package Update)
        \App\Models\AuditLog::create([
            'user_id' => null,
            'admin_id' => auth()->id(),
            'action' => 'Package Updated',
            'details' => "Updated investment package '{$package->name}' details.",
        ]);

        return redirect()->route('admin.packages.index')->with('status', 'Package updated successfully!');
    }

    /**
     * Remove the specified resource from storage.
     */
    public function destroy(Package $package)
    {
        if (!auth()->user()->hasAnyRole(['System Owner', 'Super Admin', 'super_admin']) && !auth()->user()->hasDirectPermission('manage_packages')) {
            abort(403, 'Unauthorized. You do not have the required permissions to manage packages.');
        }

        $package->delete();

        // Non-Repudiation Logging (Package Deletion)
        \App\Models\AuditLog::create([
            'user_id' => null,
            'admin_id' => auth()->id(),
            'action' => 'Package Deleted',
            'details' => "Deleted investment package '{$package->name}' permanently.",
        ]);

        return redirect()->route('admin.packages.index')->with('status', 'Package deleted successfully.');
    }
}

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Artisan;

class SystemController extends Controller
{
    public function runMigrations()
    {
        try {
            Artisan::call('migrate', ['--force' => true]);
            $output = Artisan::output();
            return back()->with('success', 'Database updated successfully! Log: ' . $output);
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            return back()->withErrors(['error' => 'Migration failed: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;

class SystemSettingController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        return view('admin.settings.index');
    }

    public function transferOwnership(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'email' => ['required', 'email', 'exists:users,email'],
        ]);

        $currentOwner = Auth::user();
        $newOwner = User::where('email', $request->email)->firstOrFail();

        if ($currentOwner->id === $newOwner->id) {
            return back()->withErrors(['email' => 'You are already the System Owner.']);
        }

        // 1. Crown the new System Owner
        $newOwner->assignRole('System Owner');

        // 2. Safely step down the current owner to a Super Admin so they aren't fully locked out
        $currentOwner->removeRole('System Owner');
        if (!$currentOwner->hasRole('Super Admin') && !$currentOwner->hasRole('super_admin')) {
            $currentOwner->assignRole('Super Admin');
        }

        // 3. Log out to finalize the security transfer
        Auth::logout();
        $request->session()->invalidate();
        $request->session()->regenerateToken();

        return redirect('/login')->with('status', 'System Ownership has been successfully transferred. You have been logged out.');
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\bolly\OneDrive\Desktop\Castle Royal Project\CastleRoyalAgro\app\Http\Controllers\Admin\TwoFactorAuthController.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use PragmaRX\Google2FA\Google2FA;
use BaconQrCode\Renderer\ImageRenderer;
use BaconQrCode\Writer;
use BaconQrCode\Renderer\Image\SvgImageBackEnd;
use BaconQrCode\Renderer\RendererStyle\RendererStyle;

class TwoFactorAuthController extends Controller
{
    public function enable(Request $request)
    {
        $user = auth()->user();
        $google2fa = new Google2FA();
        
        // Generate secret key if not already present
        if (!$user->google2fa_secret) {
            $user->google2fa_secret = $google2fa->generateSecretKey();
            $user->save(); // Save immediately to lock it in
        }

        // Generate QR Code SVG
        $g2faUrl = $google2fa->getQRCodeUrl(
            config('app.name'),
            $user->email,
            $user->google2fa_secret
        );

        $renderer = new ImageRenderer(
            new RendererStyle(200),
            new SvgImageBackEnd()
        );
        $writer = new Writer($renderer);
        $qrCodeSvg = $writer->writeString($g2faUrl);

        return view('admin.2fa.setup', [
            'secret' => $user->google2fa_secret,
            'qrCodeSvg' => $qrCodeSvg
        ]);
    }

    public function showVerify()
    {
        return view('admin.2fa.verify');
    }

    public function verify(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate(['code' => 'required|digits:6']);
        $user = auth()->user();
        $google2fa = new Google2FA();

        $valid = $google2fa->verifyKey($user->google2fa_secret, $request->code);

        if ($valid) {
            session(['2fa_verified' => true]);
            return redirect()->route('admin.dashboard');
        }

        return back()->withErrors(['code' => 'Invalid authentication code.']);
    }

    public function disable(Request $request)
    {
        $user = auth()->user();
        $user->google2fa_secret = null;
        $user->save();

        return redirect()->back()->with('status', 'Two-Factor Authentication has been disabled.');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;

class UserController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display a listing of the resource.
     */
    public function index()
    {
        $users = User::with('roles')->paginate(15);
        return view('admin.users.index', compact('users'));
    }

    /**
     * Show the form for creating a new resource.
     */
    public function create()
    {
        $roles = Role::all();
        return view('admin.users.create', compact('roles'));
    }

    /**
     * Store a newly created resource in storage.
     */
    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'email' => 'required|string|email|max:255|unique:users',
            'password' => 'required|string|min:8|confirmed',
            'role' => 'required|exists:roles,name',
        ]);

        $user = User::create([
            'name' => $request->name,
            'email' => $request->email,
            'password' => \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash::make($request->password),
        ]);

        $user->assignRole($request->role);
        $user->markEmailAsVerified(); // Auto-verify administrative staff

        return redirect()->route('users.index')->with('status', 'New staff member created successfully.');
    }

    /**
     * Show the form for editing the specified resource.
     */
    public function edit(User $user)
    {
        $roles = Role::all();
        $permissions = Permission::all();
        
        return view('admin.users.edit', compact('user', 'roles', 'permissions'));
    }

    /**
     * Update the specified resource in storage.
     */
    public function update(Request $request, User $user)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'roles' => 'array',
            'permissions' => 'array',
        ]);

        // Sync Roles
        if ($request->has('roles')) {
            $user->syncRoles($request->roles);
        } else {
            // If no roles selected, ensure we don't accidentally strip Super Admin if not allowed
            // Ideally explicit deselect
            $user->syncRoles([]);
        }

        // Sync Direct Permissions
        if ($request->has('permissions')) {
            $user->syncPermissions($request->permissions);
        } else {
            $user->syncPermissions([]);
        }

        return redirect()->route('users.index')->with('status', 'User roles and permissions updated successfully.');
    }
    public function suspend($id)
    {
        if (!auth()->user()->hasRole('System Owner')) {
            abort(403, 'Only the System Owner can suspend users.');
        }

        $user = User::findOrFail($id);
        $user->status = 'frozen';
        $user->save();

        return back()->with('status', "User {$user->name} has been suspended.");
    }

    public function restore($id)
    {
        if (!auth()->user()->hasRole('System Owner')) {
            abort(403, 'Only the System Owner can restore users.');
        }

        $user = User::findOrFail($id);
        $user->status = 'active';
        $user->save();

        return back()->with('status', "User {$user->name} has been restored.");
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Withdrawal;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class WithdrawalController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display a listing of the resource.
     */
    public function index()
    {
        $withdrawals = Withdrawal::with('user')->latest()->paginate(10);
        return view('admin.withdrawals.index', compact('withdrawals'));
    }

    /**
     * Update the specified resource in storage.
     */
    public function approve($id)
    {
        if (!auth()->user()->hasAnyRole(['System Owner', 'Super Admin', 'super_admin']) && !auth()->user()->hasDirectPermission('manage_withdrawals')) {
            abort(403, 'Unauthorized. You do not have the required permissions to approve or reject withdrawals.');
        }

        $withdrawal = Withdrawal::findOrFail($id);

        if ($withdrawal->status !== 'pending') {
            return back()->with('error', 'This request has already been processed.');
        }

        $withdrawal->update(['status' => 'approved']);

        // Send Email
        \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Mail::to($withdrawal->user->email)->send(new \App\Mail\WithdrawalApproved($withdrawal));

        // Non-Repudiation Logging (Admin Approval)
        \App\Models\AuditLog::create([
            'user_id' => $withdrawal->user_id,
            'admin_id' => auth()->id(),
            'action' => 'Withdrawal Approved',
            'details' => "Approved withdrawal of GH₵ " . number_format($withdrawal->amount, 2) . " for " . $withdrawal->user->email,
        ]);

        return redirect()->back()->with('status', 'Withdrawal approved and investor notified!');
    }

    public function reject($id)
    {
        if (!auth()->user()->hasAnyRole(['System Owner', 'Super Admin', 'super_admin']) && !auth()->user()->hasDirectPermission('manage_withdrawals')) {
            abort(403, 'Unauthorized. You do not have the required permissions to approve or reject withdrawals.');
        }

        $withdrawal = Withdrawal::findOrFail($id);

        if ($withdrawal->status !== 'pending') {
            return back()->with('error', 'This request has already been processed.');
        }

        $withdrawal->update(['status' => 'rejected']);

        // Send Email
        \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Mail::to($withdrawal->user->email)->send(new \App\Mail\WithdrawalRejected($withdrawal));

        // Non-Repudiation Logging (Admin Rejection)
        \App\Models\AuditLog::create([
            'user_id' => $withdrawal->user_id,
            'admin_id' => auth()->id(),
            'action' => 'Withdrawal Rejected',
            'details' => "Rejected withdrawal of GH₵ " . number_format($withdrawal->amount, 2) . ". Reason: Policy / Insufficient Funds.",
        ]);

        return redirect()->back()->with('status', 'Withdrawal rejected and investor notified!');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Auth\LoginRequest;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class AuthenticatedSessionController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the login view.
     */
    public function create(): View
    {
        return view('auth.login');
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming authentication request.
     */
    public function store(LoginRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->authenticate();

        $request->session()->regenerate();

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    }

    /**
     * Destroy an authenticated session.
     */
    public function destroy(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        Auth::guard('web')->logout();

        $request->session()->invalidate();

        $request->session()->regenerateToken();

        return redirect('/');
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\bolly\OneDrive\Desktop\Castle Royal Project\CastleRoyalAgro\app\Http\Controllers\Auth\ConfirmablePasswordController.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class ConfirmablePasswordController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Show the confirm password view.
     */
    public function show(): View
    {
        return view('auth.confirm-password');
    }

    /**
     * Confirm the user's password.
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        if (! Auth::guard('web')->validate([
            'email' => $request->user()->email,
            'password' => $request->password,
        ])) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'password' => __('auth.password'),
            ]);
        }

        $request->session()->put('auth.password_confirmed_at', time());

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class EmailVerificationNotificationController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Send a new email verification notification.
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        if ($request->user()->hasVerifiedEmail()) {
            return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
        }

        $request->user()->sendEmailVerificationNotification();

        return back()->with('status', 'verification-link-sent');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\PasswordReset;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Password;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class NewPasswordController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the password reset view.
     */
    public function create(Request $request): View
    {
        return view('auth.reset-password', ['request' => $request]);
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming new password request.
     *
     * @throws \Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'token' => ['required'],
            'email' => ['required', 'email'],
            'password' => ['required', 'confirmed', Rules\Password::defaults()],
        ]);

        // Here we will attempt to reset the user's password. If it is successful we
        // will update the password on an actual user model and persist it to the
        // database. Otherwise we will parse the error and return the response.
        $status = Password::reset(
            $request->only('email', 'password', 'password_confirmation', 'token'),
            function (User $user) use ($request) {
                $user->forceFill([
                    'password' => Hash::make($request->password),
                    'remember_token' => Str::random(60),
                ])->save();

                event(new PasswordReset($user));
            }
        );

        // If the password was successfully reset, we will redirect the user back to
        // the application's home authenticated view. If there is an error we can
        // redirect them back to where they came from with their error message.
        return $status == Password::PASSWORD_RESET
                    ? redirect()->route('login')->with('status', __($status))
                    : back()->withInput($request->only('email'))
                        ->withErrors(['email' => __($status)]);
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules\Password;

class PasswordController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Update the user's password.
     */
    public function update(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $validated = $request->validateWithBag('updatePassword', [
            'current_password' => ['required', 'current_password'],
            'password' => ['required', Password::defaults(), 'confirmed'],
        ]);

        $request->user()->update([
            'password' => Hash::make($validated['password']),
        ]);

        return back()->with('status', 'password-updated');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Password;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class PasswordResetLinkController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the password reset link request view.
     */
    public function create(): View
    {
        return view('auth.forgot-password');
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming password reset link request.
     *
     * @throws \Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'email' => ['required', 'email'],
        ]);

        // We will send the password reset link to this user. Once we have attempted
        // to send the link, we will examine the response then see the message we
        // need to show to the user. Finally, we'll send out a proper response.
        $status = Password::sendResetLink(
            $request->only('email')
        );

        return $status == Password::RESET_LINK_SENT
                    ? back()->with('status', __($status))
                    : back()->withInput($request->only('email'))
                        ->withErrors(['email' => __($status)]);
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Registered;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class RegisteredUserController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the registration view.
     */
    public function create(): View
    {
        return view('auth.register');
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming registration request.
     *
     * @throws \Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'email' => ['required', 'string', 'lowercase', 'email', 'max:255', 'unique:'.User::class],
            'password' => ['required', 'confirmed', Rules\Password::defaults()],
        ]);

        $user = User::create([
            'name' => $request->name,
            'email' => $request->email,
            'password' => Hash::make($request->password),
        ]);

        $user->assignRole('investor');

        try {
            event(new Registered($user));
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            // Log the error but continue logging in the user
            \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log::error('Registration Event Failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }

        Auth::login($user);

        return redirect(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Verified;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Auth\EmailVerificationRequest;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;

class VerifyEmailController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Mark the authenticated user's email address as verified.
     */
    public function __invoke(EmailVerificationRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        if ($request->user()->hasVerifiedEmail()) {
            return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false).'?verified=1');
        }

        if ($request->user()->markEmailAsVerified()) {
            event(new Verified($request->user()));
        }

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false).'?verified=1');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Investor;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Investment;
use App\Models\Package;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;

class MarketplaceController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        // 1. Fetch only 'open' or 'active' packages (adjust logic based on your Status enum)
        // Assuming status can be 'open'
        $packages = Package::whereIn('status', ['open', 'active'])->latest()->get();
        return view('investor.marketplace.index', compact('packages'));
    }

    public function show(Package $package)
    {
        return view('investor.marketplace.show', compact('package'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request, Package $package)
    {
        // 1. Validate
        $request->validate([
            'units' => 'required|integer|min:1',
            'payment_reference' => 'required|string|max:255',
        ]);

        // 2. Calculate Total
        $totalAmount = $request->units * $package->price;
        
        // Calculate expected ROI
        // ROI is percentage of total amount. e.g. 10% of 1000 = 100. Total expected = 1100? Or just profit?
        // Usually ROI amount = Principal * (roi_percentage / 100).
        // Let's store the *profit* amount as expected_roi, or the total return?
        // Let's assume expected_roi is the PROFIT amount.
        $roiAmount = $totalAmount * ($package->roi_percentage / 100);


        // 3. Create Investment
        $investment = Investment::create([
            'user_id' => Auth::id(),
            'package_id' => $package->id,
            'units' => $request->units,
            'amount_invested' => $totalAmount,
            'expected_roi' => $roiAmount, // Storing purely the profit component
            'status' => 'pending', // Waiting admin approval
            'payment_reference' => $request->payment_reference,
        ]);

        // Send Alert to Admin
        try {
            \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Mail::to('kwamerahman83@gmail.com')->send(new \App\Mail\NewInvestmentAlert($investment));
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            // Log error but don't fail the request
            \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log::error('Failed to send investment alert email: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }

        // 4. Redirect
        return redirect()->route('dashboard')->with('status', 'Investment request submitted successfully! Pending approval.');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\User;
use App\Models\Investment;
use App\Models\InvestmentPackage;
use App\Models\AuditLog;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\URL;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;

class AdminController extends Controller
{
    // 1. Show the Dashboard
    public function index()
    {
        $users = User::orderBy('created_at', 'desc')->paginate(10);
        
        $pendingInvestmentsCount = 0;
        try {
            $pendingInvestmentsCount = Investment::where('status', 'pending')->count();
        } catch (\Exception $e) {}

        // --- CTD COMMAND CENTER METRICS ---
        $totalRegisteredUsers = User::count();
        
        $totalActiveInvestments = 0;
        try {
            $totalActiveInvestments = Investment::where('status', 'active')->sum('amount');
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            $totalActiveInvestments = User::sum('total_invested');
        }

        $pendingWithdrawalsAmount = 0;
        $pendingWithdrawalsCount = 0;
        try {
            if (class_exists(\App\Models\Withdrawal::class)) {
                $pendingWithdrawalsAmount = \App\Models\Withdrawal::where('status', 'pending')->sum('amount');
                $pendingWithdrawalsCount = \App\Models\Withdrawal::where('status', 'pending')->count();
            }
        } catch (\Exception $e) {}

        // THE FIX: Use native Laravel relationships to bypass Spatie's strict exceptions
        $staffCount = User::whereHas('roles', function($query) {
            $query->whereIn('name', ['System Owner', 'Super Admin', 'Admin']);
        })->count();

        return view('admin.dashboard', compact(
            'users', 
            'pendingInvestmentsCount',
            'totalRegisteredUsers',
            'totalActiveInvestments',
            'pendingWithdrawalsAmount',
            'pendingWithdrawalsCount',
            'staffCount'
        ));
    }

    // 2. Show the "Manage User" Page
    public function show($id)
    {
        $user = User::findOrFail($id);
        $packages = InvestmentPackage::all();
        $investments = $user->investments()->orderBy('created_at', 'desc')->get(); // Fetch investments
        $auditLogs = AuditLog::where('user_id', $user->id)->latest()->take(5)->get(); // Fetch logs

        return view('admin.users.show', compact('user', 'packages', 'investments', 'auditLogs'));
    }

    // 3. Update the User (THE FIX)
    public function update(Request $request, $id)
    {
        $user = User::findOrFail($id);

        // Security: Prevent lower-level admins from editing Super Admins
        if ($user->role === 'super_admin' && Auth::user()->id !== $user->id) {
             return redirect()->back()->with('error', 'You cannot edit a Super Admin.');
        }

        // Validate inputs (Simple and Permissive)
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'role' => ['required', 'string', 'in:investor,admin,super_admin'],
            'status' => ['required', 'string', 'in:active,frozen,blocked'],
            'total_invested' => ['nullable', 'numeric', 'min:0'],
            'active_acres' => ['nullable', 'numeric', 'min:0'],
            'projected_roi' => ['nullable', 'numeric', 'min:0'],
        ]);

        // Capture Old State for Audit
        $oldRole = $user->role;
        $oldStatus = $user->status;

        // Explicitly Update Fields
        $user->role = $request->role;
        $user->status = $request->status;
        
        // THE FIX: Use 'input' with a default value of 0
        $user->total_invested = $request->input('total_invested', 0);
        $user->active_acres = $request->input('active_acres', 0);
        $user->projected_roi = $request->input('projected_roi', 0);

        $user->save();

        // Audit Log if Role or Status Changed
        if ($oldRole !== $user->role) {
            AuditLog::create([
                'user_id' => $user->id,
                'admin_id' => Auth::id(),
                'action' => 'Role Updated',
                'details' => "Changed role from $oldRole to {$user->role}",
            ]);
        }
        if ($oldStatus !== $user->status) {
            AuditLog::create([
                'user_id' => $user->id,
                'admin_id' => Auth::id(),
                'action' => 'Status Updated',
                'details' => "Changed status from $oldStatus to {$user->status}",
            ]);
        }

        return redirect()->route('admin.users.show', $user->id)
            ->with('status', 'User updated successfully!');
    }

    // 4. Add New Investment (Receipt)
    public function invest(Request $request, $id)
    {
        $user = User::findOrFail($id);

        $validated = $request->validate([
            'amount' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'acres' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'roi_percentage' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'projected_profit' => 'nullable|numeric|min:0', 
            'package_name' => 'nullable|string|max:255',
        ]);

        // 1. Calculate Profit based on Input Percentage
        // Note: Adding support for 'projected_profit' input if available (e.g. from package logic), otherwise calc.
        // Actually, let's strictly calculate based on ROI % provided to ensure consistency.
        $profit = $request->amount * ($request->roi_percentage / 100);

        // 2. Create the Receipt
        $investment = $user->investments()->create([
            'package_name' => $request->package_name,
            'amount' => $request->amount,
            'acres' => $request->acres,
            'roi_percentage' => $request->roi_percentage, 
            'projected_profit' => $profit,
            'status' => 'active',
        ]);

        // 3. Update User Totals
        $user->increment('total_invested', $request->amount);
        $user->increment('active_acres', $request->acres);
        $user->increment('projected_roi', ($request->amount + $profit)); // Total Cash Return

        // 4. Audit Log
        AuditLog::create([
            'user_id' => $user->id,
            'admin_id' => Auth::id(),
            'action' => 'Investment Added',
            'details' => "Added Investment of GH₵ {$request->amount} ({$request->acres} acres).",
        ]);

        return redirect()->back()->with('status', 'Investment added successfully!');
    }

    /**
     * Force reset a user's password.
     */
    public function resetPassword(Request $request, $id)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'new_password' => 'required|string|min:6',
        ]);

        $cleanPassword = trim($request->new_password);
        
        // DIRECT DB UPDATE (Bypass Model/Mutators)
        DB::table('users')
            ->where('id', $id)
            ->update([
                'password' => Hash::make($cleanPassword)
            ]);

        return back()->with('success', "SUCCESS: Password force-updated to '{$cleanPassword}'.");
    }

    /**
     * Generate a Magic Link
     */
    public function generateMagicLink($id)
    {
        $user = User::findOrFail($id);

        // standard generation (automatically adds signature & expires as query params)
        $url = URL::temporarySignedRoute(
            'magic.login', 
            now()->addMinutes(30), 
            ['user' => $user->id]
        );

        return back()->with('success', $url);
    }

    /**
     * Consume Magic Link
     */
    public function loginWithMagicLink($id)
    {
        $user = User::findOrFail($id);
    
        Auth::login($user);
    
        return redirect('/dashboard')->with('status', 'Magic Login Successful! You are now logged in.');
    }

    // 6. Update Existing Investment
    public function updateInvestment(Request $request, $id)
    {
        $investment = Investment::findOrFail($id);
        $user = $investment->user;

        $request->validate([
            'amount' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'acres' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'roi_percentage' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
        ]);

        // Calculate differences
        $amountDiff = $request->amount - $investment->amount;
        $acresDiff = $request->acres - $investment->acres;
        
        // New Profit
        $newProfit = $request->amount * ($request->roi_percentage / 100);
        $oldProfit = $investment->projected_profit;
        $profitDiff = $newProfit - $oldProfit;
        
        // Update Investment
        $investment->update([
            'amount' => $request->amount,
            'acres' => $request->acres,
            'roi_percentage' => $request->roi_percentage,
            'projected_profit' => $newProfit,
        ]);

        // Update User Totals
        if ($amountDiff != 0) $user->increment('total_invested', $amountDiff);
        if ($acresDiff != 0) $user->increment('active_acres', $acresDiff);
        
        // Update Projected ROI (Principal + Profit)
        // Total Diff = (NewPrincipal + NewProfit) - (OldPrincipal + OldProfit)
        //            = (AmountDiff) + (ProfitDiff)
        $totalRoiDiff = $amountDiff + $profitDiff;
        if ($totalRoiDiff != 0) $user->increment('projected_roi', $totalRoiDiff);

        // Audit Log
        AuditLog::create([
            'user_id' => $user->id,
            'admin_id' => Auth::id(),
            'action' => 'Investment Updated',
            'details' => "Updated Investment #{$investment->id}. Amount: {$amountDiff}, Acres: {$acresDiff}.",
        ]);

        return redirect()->back()->with('status', 'Investment updated successfully!');
    }

    // 7. Delete Investment (Soft Delete)
    public function destroyInvestment($id)
    {
        $investment = Investment::findOrFail($id);
        $user = $investment->user;

        // Recalculate User Totals (Subtract)
        $user->decrement('total_invested', $investment->amount);
        $user->decrement('active_acres', $investment->acres);
        $user->decrement('projected_roi', ($investment->amount + $investment->projected_profit));

        // Audit Log
        AuditLog::create([
            'user_id' => $user->id,
            'admin_id' => Auth::id(),
            'action' => 'Investment Deleted',
            'details' => "Deleted Investment #{$investment->id} of GH₵ {$investment->amount}.",
        ]);

        $investment->delete();

        return redirect()->back()->with('status', 'Investment deleted (soft) and totals adjusted.');
    }
    // 8. Bulk ROI Adjustment (Hybrid: Yield Per Acre OR Financial ROI)
    public function bulkUpdateRoi(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'start_date'        => 'required|date',
            'end_date'          => 'required|date|after_or_equal:start_date',
            'calculation_method'=> 'required|in:per_acre,percentage',
            'declared_value'    => 'required|numeric|min:0',
        ]);

        // 1. Find Investments in Range
        $investments = \App\Models\Investment::whereBetween('created_at', [
            $request->start_date . ' 00:00:00',
            $request->end_date . ' 23:59:59'
        ])->get();

        $count = $investments->count();
        $userIdsToSync = [];

        // 2. Loop & Update based on Method
        foreach ($investments as $inv) {
            
            if ($request->calculation_method === 'percentage') {
                // Method A: Financial (ROI %)
                $roi_percent = $request->declared_value;
                $profit = $inv->amount * ($roi_percent / 100);

            } else {
                // Method B: Yield (GHS / Acre)
                $profit = $inv->acres * $request->declared_value;
                
                // Back-calculate % for record keeping
                $roi_percent = ($inv->amount > 0) ? ($profit / $inv->amount) * 100 : 0;
            }

            $inv->roi_percentage = $roi_percent;
            $inv->projected_profit = $profit;
            $inv->save();

            if (!in_array($inv->user_id, $userIdsToSync)) {
                $userIdsToSync[] = $inv->user_id;
            }
        }

        // 3. Sync Users
        foreach ($userIdsToSync as $userId) {
            $user = User::find($userId);
            if ($user) {
                // Sum of all projected profits for this user
                $totalProfit = $user->investments()->sum('projected_profit');
                
                // Projected Cash Return = Principal + Profit
                $totalInvested = $user->investments()->sum('amount');
                $user->projected_roi = $totalInvested + $totalProfit;
                $user->save();
            }
        }

        return redirect()->back()->with('status', "Success! Updated {$count} investments using method: {$request->calculation_method}.");
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\bolly\OneDrive\Desktop\Castle Royal Project\CastleRoyalAgro\app\Http\Controllers\AdminWithdrawalController.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Withdrawal;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Mail\WithdrawalApproved;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Mail;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class AdminWithdrawalController extends Controller
{
    public function index() {
        $withdrawals = \App\Models\Withdrawal::with('user')->latest()->paginate(10);
        return view('admin.withdrawals.index', compact('withdrawals'));
    }

    public function approve(Request $request, $id)
    {
        // Inject the approved status and forward to the existing update logic
        $request->merge(['status' => 'approved']);
        return $this->update($request, $id);
    }

    public function reject(Request $request, $id)
    {
        // Inject the rejected status and forward to the existing update logic
        $request->merge(['status' => 'rejected']);
        return $this->update($request, $id);
    }

    public function update(Request $request, $id)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'status' => 'required|in:approved,rejected',
        ]);

        $withdrawal = Withdrawal::findOrFail($id);

        if ($withdrawal->status !== 'pending') {
            return back()->with('error', 'This request has already been processed.');
        }

        if ($request->status === 'approved') {
            // Check balance again just in case
            if ($withdrawal->user->projected_roi < $withdrawal->amount) {
                return back()->with('error', 'User has insufficient funds to approve this withdrawal.');
            }

            // Deduct from User's Projected ROI
            $withdrawal->user->decrement('projected_roi', $withdrawal->amount);
        }

        $withdrawal->update([
            'status' => $request->status,
            'admin_note' => $request->admin_note, // Optional note
        ]);

        if ($request->status === 'approved') {
            // Send Approval Email via Direct API
            Http::withHeaders([
                'api-key' => env('BREVO_API_KEY'),
            ])->post('https://api.brevo.com/v3/smtp/email', [
                'sender' => ['email' => env('MAIL_FROM_ADDRESS'), 'name' => 'Castle Royal Admin'],
                'to' => [['email' => $withdrawal->user->email, 'name' => $withdrawal->user->name]],
                'subject' => '🎉 Withdrawal Approved!',
                'htmlContent' => "<h2>Hello {$withdrawal->user->name},</h2><p>Your withdrawal of <strong>GHS " . number_format($withdrawal->amount, 2) . "</strong> has been approved and processed.</p><p>Thank you for investing with Castle Royal Agro.</p>"
            ]);

            // Log Action in Super Admin Audit Log
            \App\Models\AuditLog::create([
                'user_id' => $withdrawal->user_id,
                'admin_id' => auth()->id(),
                'action' => 'Withdrawal Approved',
                'details' => "Approved withdrawal of GH₵ " . number_format($withdrawal->amount, 2) . " for " . ($withdrawal->user->email ?? 'investor') . ".",
            ]);

            return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Withdrawal Approved & Investor Notified.');
        } 
        elseif ($request->status === 'rejected') {
            // Send Denial Email via Direct API
            Http::withHeaders([
                'api-key' => env('BREVO_API_KEY'),
            ])->post('https://api.brevo.com/v3/smtp/email', [
                'sender' => ['email' => env('MAIL_FROM_ADDRESS'), 'name' => 'Castle Royal Admin'],
                'to' => [['email' => $withdrawal->user->email, 'name' => $withdrawal->user->name]],
                'subject' => '⚠️ Withdrawal Request Declined',
                'htmlContent' => "<h2>Hello {$withdrawal->user->name},</h2><p>Your withdrawal request for <strong>GHS " . number_format($withdrawal->amount, 2) . "</strong> has been declined.</p><p>Please check your payment details in your dashboard or contact support for more information.</p>"
            ]);

            // Log Action in Super Admin Audit Log
            \App\Models\AuditLog::create([
                'user_id' => $withdrawal->user_id,
                'admin_id' => auth()->id(),
                'action' => 'Withdrawal Rejected',
                'details' => "Rejected withdrawal of GH₵ " . number_format($withdrawal->amount, 2) . " for " . ($withdrawal->user->email ?? 'investor') . ".",
            ]);

            return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Withdrawal Denied & Investor Notified.');
        }

        return back()->with('status', 'Withdrawal request updated successfully.');
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\bolly\OneDrive\Desktop\Castle Royal Project\CastleRoyalAgro\app\Http\Controllers\DashboardController.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;

class DashboardController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        // 1. Get the currently logged-in user
        $user = Auth::user();

        // Calculate Financials from the User Model
        // 1. Total Invested: Sum of ACTIVE investments
        $totalInvested = $user->investments()->where('status', 'active')->sum('amount_invested');

        // 2. Active Acres: Sum of units (acres) of ACTIVE investments
        // Note: 'units' column holds the acres/quantity
        $activeAcres = $user->investments()->where('status', 'active')->sum('units');

        // 3. Projected ROI: Unify Legacy and New Package Returns
        $legacyProjectedReturn = $user->projected_roi ?? 0.0;
        $packageRoi = $user->investments()->where('status', 'active')->sum('expected_roi');
        
        // Unified Total Projected Return
        $projectedRoi = $legacyProjectedReturn + $packageRoi;

        // 4. Total Withdrawn: Sum of APPROVED withdrawals (For Display)
        $totalWithdrawn = $user->withdrawals()->where('status', 'approved')->sum('amount');

        // 5. Total Amount Left (Available Balance)
        // This represents the true remaining funds: (Unified ROI - [Approved + Pending Withdrawals])
        $unavailableFunds = $user->withdrawals()->whereIn('status', ['approved', 'pending'])->sum('amount');
        $totalAmountLeft = max(0, $projectedRoi - $unavailableFunds);

        // Fetch Investment History
        $investments = $user->investments()->with('package')->latest()->get();

        return view('dashboard', compact('user', 'totalInvested', 'activeAcres', 'projectedRoi', 'totalWithdrawn', 'totalAmountLeft', 'investments'));
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\InvestmentPackage;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;

class InvestmentPackageController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display a listing of the resource.
     */
    public function index() {
        $packages = \App\Models\InvestmentPackage::paginate(10);
        return view('admin.packages.index', compact('packages'));
    }

    /**
     * Store a newly created resource in storage.
     */
    public function store(\Illuminate\Http\Request $request) {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'description' => 'nullable|string',
            'price' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'roi_percentage' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'duration_months' => 'required|integer|min:1',
            'status' => 'required|string',
            'image' => 'nullable|file|max:5120',
            'image_url' => 'nullable|string'
        ]);

        $validated['slug'] = Str::slug($validated['name']);

        if ($request->hasFile('image')) {
            $validated['image'] = $request->file('image')->store('packages', 'public');
        } elseif (!empty($validated['image_url'])) {
            $validated['image'] = $validated['image_url'];
        }

        unset($validated['image_url']);

        \App\Models\InvestmentPackage::create($validated);

        return redirect()->route('admin.packages.index')->with('status', 'Package created successfully.');
    }

    /**
     * Remove the specified resource from storage.
     */
    public function destroy(string $id)
    {
        $package = InvestmentPackage::findOrFail($id);
        
        // Optional: delete image from storage if it exists and is not a URL
        if ($package->image && !filter_var($package->image, FILTER_VALIDATE_URL)) {
            Storage::disk('public')->delete($package->image);
        }

        $package->delete();

        return redirect()->back()->with('status', 'Package deleted successfully.');
    }

    public function create()
    {
        return view('admin.packages.create');
    }

    public function edit(string $id)
    {
        $package = \App\Models\InvestmentPackage::findOrFail($id);
        return view('admin.packages.edit', compact('package'));
    }

    public function update(\Illuminate\Http\Request $request, string $id) {
        $package = \App\Models\InvestmentPackage::findOrFail($id);

        $validated = $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'description' => 'nullable|string',
            'price' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'roi_percentage' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'duration_months' => 'required|integer|min:1',
            'status' => 'required|string',
            'image' => 'nullable|file|max:5120',
            'image_url' => 'nullable|string'
        ]);

        $validated['slug'] = Str::slug($validated['name']);

        if ($request->hasFile('image')) {
            // Delete old image if it's stored locally
            if ($package->image && !filter_var($package->image, FILTER_VALIDATE_URL)) {
                Storage::disk('public')->delete($package->image);
            }
            $validated['image'] = $request->file('image')->store('packages', 'public');
        } elseif (!empty($validated['image_url'])) {
            $validated['image'] = $validated['image_url'];
        } else {
             // Keep the existing image if no new one or url is provided
             $validated['image'] = $package->image;
        }

        unset($validated['image_url']);

        $package->update($validated);

        return redirect()->route('admin.packages.index')->with('status', 'Package updated successfully.');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Http\Requests\ProfileUpdateRequest;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Redirect;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class ProfileController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the user's profile form.
     */
    public function edit(Request $request): View
    {
        return view('profile.edit', [
            'user' => $request->user(),
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Update the user's profile information.
     */
    public function update(ProfileUpdateRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->user()->fill($request->validated());

        if ($request->user()->isDirty('email')) {
            $request->user()->email_verified_at = null;
        }

        $request->user()->save();

        return Redirect::route('profile.edit')->with('status', 'profile-updated');
    }

    /**
     * Update the user's payment details.
     */
    public function updatePaymentDetails(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'momo_details' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:500'],
            'bank_details' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:500'],
        ]);

        $request->user()->update($validated);

        return Redirect::route('dashboard')->with('status', 'Wallet details updated successfully!');
    }

    /**
     * Delete the user's account.
     */
    public function destroy(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validateWithBag('userDeletion', [
            'password' => ['required', 'current_password'],
        ]);

        $user = $request->user();

        Auth::logout();

        $user->delete();

        $request->session()->invalidate();
        $request->session()->regenerateToken();

        return Redirect::to('/');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use App\Services\BrevoMailService;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;

class SupportController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Handle the contact support form submission.
     */
    public function handleContact(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'email' => 'required|email|max:255',
            'subject' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'message' => 'required|string',
        ]);

        try {
            // Send email to Admin
            $adminEmail = env('MAIL_FROM_ADDRESS');
            $subject = '📩 New Support Request: ' . $request->subject;
            
            $htmlContent = "
                <div style='font-family: Arial; padding: 20px; border: 1px solid #eee;'>
                    <h2>New Support Message</h2>
                    <p><strong>From:</strong> {$request->name} ({$request->email})</p>
                    <p><strong>Subject:</strong> {$request->subject}</p>
                    <hr>
                    <p><strong>Message:</strong></p>
                    <p>" . nl2br(e($request->message)) . "</p>
                </div>
            ";

            BrevoMailService::send($adminEmail, 'Castle Royal Support', $subject, $htmlContent);

            return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Your message has been sent to our support team! 🚀');

        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            Log::error('Support Email Failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
            return redirect()->back()->with('error', 'Sorry, something went wrong. Please try again later.');
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\bolly\OneDrive\Desktop\Castle Royal Project\CastleRoyalAgro\app\Http\Controllers\WithdrawalController.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Withdrawal;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;

class WithdrawalController extends Controller
{
    public function create()
    {
        $user = Auth::user();

        // 1. Get Total Projected Return (Unified)
        $legacyProjectedReturn = $user->projected_roi ?? 0.0;
        $packageRoi = \App\Models\Investment::where('user_id', $user->id)
            ->where('status', 'active') // Only count active, approved investments
            ->sum('expected_roi');

        $projectedReturn = $legacyProjectedReturn + $packageRoi;

        // 2. Get Total Withdrawn AND Pending (to prevent spamming requests)
        $unavailableFunds = Withdrawal::where('user_id', $user->id)
            ->whereIn('status', ['approved', 'pending'])
            ->sum('amount');

        // 3. Calculate True Available Balance
        $availableBalance = max(0, $projectedReturn - $unavailableFunds);

        return view('withdrawals.create', compact('availableBalance'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $user = Auth::user();

        // 1. Get Total Projected Return (Unified)
        $legacyProjectedReturn = $user->projected_roi ?? 0.0;
        $packageRoi = \App\Models\Investment::where('user_id', $user->id)
            ->where('status', 'active') // Only count active, approved investments
            ->sum('expected_roi');

        $projectedReturn = $legacyProjectedReturn + $packageRoi;

        // 2. Get Total Withdrawn AND Pending (to prevent spamming requests)
        $unavailableFunds = Withdrawal::where('user_id', $user->id)
            ->whereIn('status', ['approved', 'pending'])
            ->sum('amount');

        // 3. Calculate True Available Balance
        $availableBalance = max(0, $projectedReturn - $unavailableFunds);

        $request->validate([
            'amount' => ['required', 'numeric', 'min:1', 'max:' . $availableBalance],
            'payment_details' => 'required|string',
        ], [
            'amount.max' => 'You cannot withdraw more than your available balance of GH₵ ' . number_format($availableBalance, 2),
        ]);

        Withdrawal::create([
            'user_id' => $user->id,
            'amount' => $request->amount,
            'payment_details' => $request->payment_details,
            'status' => 'pending',
        ]);

        // Non-Repudiation Logging (Investor Request)
        \App\Models\AuditLog::create([
            'user_id' => $user->id,
            'admin_id' => $user->id, // HOTFIX: Bypass NOT NULL constraint by logging self as admin
            'action' => 'Withdrawal Requested',
            'details' => "Investor requested withdrawal of GH₵ " . number_format($request->amount, 2) . " via " . $request->payment_details,
        ]);

        return redirect()->route('dashboard')->with('status', 'Withdrawal request submitted successfully.');
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\bolly\OneDrive\Desktop\Castle Royal Project\CastleRoyalAgro\app\Http\Middleware\EnsureAccountIsActive.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use Closure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;

class EnsureAccountIsActive
{
    /**
     * Handle an incoming request.
     *
     * @param  \Closure(\Illuminate\Http\Request): (\Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response)  $next
     */
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    {
        if (Auth::check()) {
            $user = Auth::user();
            
            // STRICTLY PERMISSIVE: Only deny if explicitly 'banned'.
            // Allow null, allow 'active', allow 'frozen' (unless strictly banned).
            if ($user->status === 'banned') {
                Auth::logout();

                $request->session()->invalidate();
                $request->session()->regenerateToken();

                return redirect()->route('login')->withErrors([
                    'email' => 'Your account is currently banned. Please contact support.',
                ]);
            }
        }

        return $next($request);
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use Closure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;

class EnsureUserIsAdmin
{
    /**
     * Handle an incoming request.
     *
     * @param  \Closure(\Illuminate\Http\Request): (\Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response)  $next
     */
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    {
        if (Auth::check() && (Auth::user()->role === 'admin' || Auth::user()->role === 'super_admin')) {
            return $next($request);
        }

        return redirect()->route('dashboard')->with('error', 'Unauthorized access.');
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\bolly\OneDrive\Desktop\Castle Royal Project\CastleRoyalAgro\app\Http\Middleware\RequireTwoFactor.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use Closure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;

class RequireTwoFactor
{
    /**
     * Handle an incoming request.
     *
     * @param  \Closure(\Illuminate\Http\Request): (\Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response)  $next
     */
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    {
        $user = auth()->user();

        if ($user) {
            // 1. If user hasn't set up 2FA yet, force them to setup
            if (!$user->google2fa_secret) {
                // Prevent infinite redirect loop if already on setup page or enable action
                if (!$request->routeIs('admin.2fa.setup') && !$request->routeIs('admin.2fa.enable') && !$request->routeIs('admin.2fa.verify.store')) {
                    return redirect()->route('admin.2fa.setup');
                }
            } 
            // 2. If user has 2FA set up, check if session is verified
            elseif (!$request->session()->has('2fa_verified')) {
                // Prevent infinite redirect loop if already on verify page or verify action
                if (!$request->routeIs('admin.2fa.verify') && !$request->routeIs('admin.2fa.verify.store')) {
                    return redirect()->route('admin.2fa.verify');
                }
            }
        }

        return $next($request);
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\bolly\OneDrive\Desktop\Castle Royal Project\CastleRoyalAgro\app\Http\Requests\Auth\LoginRequest.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Requests\Auth;

use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Lockout;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Http\FormRequest;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\RateLimiter;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

class LoginRequest extends FormRequest
{
    /**
     * Determine if the user is authorized to make this request.
     */
    public function authorize(): bool
    {
        return true;
    }

    /**
     * Get the validation rules that apply to the request.
     *
     * @return array<string, \Illuminate\Contracts\Validation\ValidationRule|array<mixed>|string>
     */
    public function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'email' => ['required', 'string', 'email'],
            'password' => ['required', 'string'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Attempt to authenticate the request's credentials.
     *
     * @throws \Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException
     */
    public function authenticate(): void
    {
        $this->ensureIsNotRateLimited();

        if (! Auth::attempt($this->only('email', 'password'), $this->boolean('remember'))) {
            RateLimiter::hit($this->throttleKey());

            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'email' => trans('auth.failed'),
            ]);
        }

        RateLimiter::clear($this->throttleKey());
    }

    /**
     * Ensure the login request is not rate limited.
     *
     * @throws \Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException
     */
    public function ensureIsNotRateLimited(): void
    {
        if (! RateLimiter::tooManyAttempts($this->throttleKey(), 5)) {
            return;
        }

        event(new Lockout($this));

        $seconds = RateLimiter::availableIn($this->throttleKey());

        throw ValidationException::withMessages([
            'email' => trans('auth.throttle', [
                'seconds' => $seconds,
                'minutes' => ceil($seconds / 60),
            ]),
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Get the rate limiting throttle key for the request.
     */
    public function throttleKey(): string
    {
        return Str::transliterate(Str::lower($this->string('email')).'|'.$this->ip());
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\bolly\OneDrive\Desktop\Castle Royal Project\CastleRoyalAgro\app\Models\AuditLog.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use App\Models\User;

class AuditLog extends Model
{
    use HasFactory;

    protected $fillable = [
        'user_id',
        'admin_id',
        'action',
        'details',
    ];

    public function admin()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'admin_id');
    }

    public function user()
    {

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Contracts\Auth\MustVerifyEmail;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Spatie\Permission\Traits\HasRoles;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Auth\User as Authenticatable;
use Illuminate\Notifications\Notifiable;

class User extends Authenticatable implements MustVerifyEmail
{
    /** @use HasFactory<\Database\Factories\UserFactory> */
    use HasRoles, HasFactory, Notifiable;

    /**
     * Get the investments for the user.
     */
    public function investments()
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Investment::class);
    }

    public function withdrawals()
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Withdrawal::class)->latest();
    }

    /**
     * The attributes that are mass assignable.
     *
     * @var list<string>
     */
    protected $fillable = [
        'name',
        'email',
        'password',
        'role',
        'status',
        'phone',
        'ghana_card_id',
        'address',
        'next_of_kin_name',
        'next_of_kin_phone',
        'total_invested',
        'active_acres',
        'projected_roi',
        'momo_details',
        'bank_details',
        'google2fa_secret', // Added for 2FA
    ];

    /**
     * The attributes that should be hidden for serialization.
     *
     * @var list<string>
     */
    protected $hidden = [
        'password',
        'remember_token',
        'google2fa_secret', // Added for 2FA
    ];

    /**
     * Get the attributes that should be cast.
     *
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'email_verified_at' => 'datetime',
        ];
    }

    /**
     * The "booted" method of the model.
     */
    protected static function booted(): void
    {
        static::created(function (User $user) {
            
            // 1. Send Welcome Email to User
            \App\Services\BrevoMailService::send(
                $user->email,
                $user->name,
                '🌱 Welcome to Castle Royal Agro!',

                    <p>Thank you for joining <strong>Castle Royal Agro</strong>. We are excited to have you on board.</p>
                    <p>You can now log in to your dashboard to view investment packages and track your portfolio.</p>
                    <br>
                    <a href='" . route('login') . "' style='background-color: #2d3748; color: white; padding: 10px 20px; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 5px;'>Login to Dashboard</a>
                </div>"
            );

            // 2. Send New Sign-up Alert to Admin
            \App\Services\BrevoMailService::send(
                env('MAIL_FROM_ADDRESS'), // Send to Admin
                'Castle Royal Admin',
                '🔔 New User Registration',
                "
                <div style='font-family: Arial; padding: 20px; border: 1px solid #eee;'>
                    <h2>New User Alert</h2>
                    <p>A new user has just registered on the platform.</p>
                    <ul>
                        <li><strong>Name:</strong> {$user->name}</li>
                        <li><strong>Email:</strong> {$user->email}</li>
                        <li><strong>Time:</strong> " . now()->format('Y-m-d H:i:s') . "</li>
                    </ul>
                    <a href='" . route('admin.dashboard') . "'>View in Admin Panel</a>

=== FILE: C:\Users\bolly\OneDrive\Desktop\Castle Royal Project\CastleRoyalAgro\app\Providers\AppServiceProvider.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Providers;

use Illuminate\Support\ServiceProvider;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\URL; // We need this tool

class AppServiceProvider extends ServiceProvider
{
    /**
     * Register any application services.
     */
    public function register(): void
    {
        
    }

    /**
     * Bootstrap any application services.
     */
    public function boot(): void
    {
        // FORCE HTTPS GLOBALLY (Fixes Signature Mismatch) -- only in production/staging
        if (! $this->app->environment('local')) {
            URL::forceScheme('https');
        }

        // Register Brevo Mailer Driver (Bulletproof)
        \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Mail::extend('brevo', function () {
            // 1. Force read from environment to bypass config cache on Render
            $key = env('BREVO_KEY') ?: config('services.brevo.key');

            // 2. Clear error if the key is missing from Render
            if (empty($key)) {
                throw new \Exception("CRITICAL: BREVO_KEY is missing from Render Environment Variables.");
            }

            // 3. Build the Symfony Transport
            return (new \Symfony\Component\Mailer\Bridge\Brevo\Transport\BrevoTransportFactory)->create(
                new \Symfony\Component\Mailer\Transport\Dsn('brevo+api', 'default', $key)
            );
        });


        // Implicitly grant "Super Admin" role all permissions
        // This works in the app by using gate-related functions like auth()->user->can() and @can()
        \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Gate::before(function ($user, $ability) {
            return $user->hasRole('super_admin') ? true : null;
        });
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\bolly\OneDrive\Desktop\Castle Royal Project\CastleRoyalAgro\app\Services\BrevoMailService.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Services;

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;

class BrevoMailService
{
    /**
     * Send an email via Brevo API (Direct HTTP)
     *
     * @param string $toEmail Recipient Email
     * @param string $toName Recipient Name
     * @param string $subject Email Subject
     * @param string $htmlContent HTML Body
     * @return bool True if successful, False otherwise
     */
    public static function send($toEmail, $toName, $subject, $htmlContent)
    {
        try {
            $response = Http::withHeaders([
                'api-key' => env('BREVO_API_KEY'),
                'Content-Type' => 'application/json',
                'Accept' => 'application/json',
            ])->post('https://api.brevo.com/v3/smtp/email', [
                'sender' => [
                    'name' => env('MAIL_FROM_NAME', 'Castle Royal Agro'),
                    'email' => env('MAIL_FROM_ADDRESS')
                ],
                'to' => [
                    [
                        'email' => $toEmail,
                        'name' => $toName
                    ]
                ],
                'subject' => $subject,
                'htmlContent' => $htmlContent
            ]);

            if ($response->successful()) {
                Log::info("Brevo Email Sent to {$toEmail}: {$subject}");
                return true;
            } else {
                Log::error("Brevo API Error (To: {$toEmail}): " . $response->body());
                return false;
            }

        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            Log::error("Brevo API Exception: " . $e->getMessage());
            return false;
        }
    }
}

            <script>
                function applyPackage() {
                    const select = document.getElementById('packageSelect');
                    const option = select.options[select.selectedIndex];
                    
                    if (option.value) {
                        document.getElementById('amount').value = option.getAttribute('data-amount');
                        document.getElementById('roi_percentage').value = option.getAttribute('data-roi');
                        document.getElementById('acres').value = option.getAttribute('data-acres');
                        document.getElementById('package_name').value = option.getAttribute('data-name');
                    }
                }

                function openEditModal(id, amount, acres, roi) {
                    const form = document.getElementById('editForm');
                    form.action = '/admin/investments/' + id; // Assuming this route exists
                    
                    document.getElementById('edit_amount').value = amount;
                    document.getElementById('edit_acres').value = acres;
                    document.getElementById('edit_roi').value = roi;
                    
                    document.getElementById('editModal').classList.remove('hidden');
                }

                function closeEditModal() {
                    document.getElementById('editModal').classList.add('hidden');
                }
            </script>

        </div>
    </div>
</x-app-layout>
